AT2026-11-08#
party document Public statement

Stillstand inakzeptabel: Wie und mit wem soll es in Regierung weitergehen?

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz criticized the ÖVP-SPÖ coalition government for creating chaos through announced ministerial reshuffles without a clear plan, called on President Van der Bellen to intervene, and demanded rapid new elections with Herbert Kickl ready to assume responsibility.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Das Chaos in der SPÖ war anscheinend noch nicht groß genug. Jetzt haben ÖVP und SPÖ gleich auf zwei von drei Seiten dieser Verlierer-Ampel das nächste Chaos losgetreten.
The chaos in the SPÖ was apparently not big enough. Now ÖVP and SPÖ have triggered the next chaos on two of three sides of this loser traffic-light coalition.
Michael Schnedlitz
Dieser politische Wahnsinn mit Endprodukt Stillstand ist vollkommen inakzeptabel!
This political madness with the end product of standstill is completely unacceptable!
Michael Schnedlitz

AT2026-11-08#
party document Public statement

Impfpflicht: Meinl-Reisinger muss sofort sämtliche Fakten zur Abstimmung offenlegen

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz demands that Foreign Minister Beate Meinl-Reisinger immediately disclose all facts about NEOS's 2022 vote supporting mandatory COVID-19 vaccination, following allegations by NEOS co-founder Veit Dengler that the decision was made against the party's internal majority.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Da geht es nicht um irgendeine vergessene Parteisitzung. Die Impfpflicht war ein schwerwiegender Eingriff in die persönliche Freiheit, der bis heute viele Menschen beschäftigt und unfassbaren Schaden angerichtet hat.
This is not about some forgotten party meeting. The mandatory vaccination was a serious infringement on personal freedom that still concerns many people today and has caused unimaginable damage.
Michael Schnedlitz
Die Ministerin soll unverzüglich erklären, wer wann welche Entscheidung getroffen hat, ob es Abstimmungen oder Beschlüsse gab und wie mit abweichenden Meinungen umgegangen wurde.
The minister should immediately explain who made which decision when, whether there were votes or resolutions, and how dissenting opinions were handled.
Michael Schnedlitz

AT2026-11-08#
party document Public statement

Pensionisten zahlen mehr, bekommen weniger: Sozialer Offenbarungseid der Regierung

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz criticized the coalition government for introducing an E-Card fee for pensioners, increasing health insurance contributions, and cutting health services, demanding the reversal of these measures or the government's resignation.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Diese Verlierer-Ampel hat beim Sparen anscheinend eine Lieblingsadresse: die Geldbörsen unserer Pensionisten! Zuerst werden ihre Krankenversicherungsbeiträge erhöht, jetzt wird ihnen auch noch die E-Card-Gebühr aufgebrummt. Wer jahrzehntelang gearbeitet und Beiträge bezahlt hat, bekommt von Christian Stocker, Markus Marterbauer und Beate Meinl-Reisinger die nächste Rechnung präsentiert. Das ist keine soziale Politik, das ist Abkassieren bei jenen, die dieses Land aufgebaut haben!
This loser traffic-light coalition apparently has a favorite address when it comes to saving: the wallets of our pensioners! First their health insurance contributions are increased, now they are also burdened with the E-Card fee. Those who have worked for decades and paid contributions are presented with the next bill by Christian Stocker, Markus Marterbauer and Beate Meinl-Reisinger. This is not social policy, this is cashing in on those who built this country!
Michael Schnedlitz
Mehr einzahlen, weniger herausbekommen – das ist das Ergebnis dieser angeblich sozialen Regierung! Dafür hat es anscheinend die SPÖ gebraucht. Wer eine Zahnprothese benötigt, zahlt mehr, und Familien verlieren im Krankheitsfall Unterstützung. Ausgerechnet unter der roten Gesundheitsministerin Korinna Schumann werden Leistungen zusammengestrichen. Die viel beschworene soziale Handschrift der SPÖ ist in Wahrheit ein Griff in die Taschen der Versicherten!
Pay more in, get less out – that is the result of this supposedly social government! Apparently that's what the SPÖ was needed for. Those who need dentures pay more, and families lose support in case of illness. It is precisely under the red Health Minister Korinna Schumann that services are being cut. The much-invoked social signature of the SPÖ is in reality a reach into the pockets of the insured!
Michael Schnedlitz

CH2026-11-03#
party document Policy proposal

Stimmfreigabe zur Feuerwerksinitiative

The Swiss Party of Labour's Central Committee decided to issue a neutral voting recommendation on the fireworks initiative, citing both ecological and safety arguments in favor and concerns about restricting working-class culture and potential police repression against the initiative.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Gemäss dem Zentralkomitee der Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz (PdAS) gibt es sowohl überzeugende Argumente für als auch gegen die Initiative. Deshalb hat das Zentralkomitee es vorgezogen, die Stimmfreigabe zu beschliessen.
According to the Central Committee of the Swiss Party of Labour (PdAS), there are convincing arguments both for and against the initiative. Therefore, the Central Committee has decided to issue a neutral voting recommendation.
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz
Als Partei, die die arbeitende Klasse organisieren will, um für ihre Interessen zu kämpfen, sollten wir die Stadien nicht den anderen überlassen und den Fans nicht durch weitere ungerechtfertigte Verbote zusätzliche Einschränkungen auferlegen.
As a party that wants to organize the working class to fight for their interests, we should not leave the stadiums to others and should not impose additional restrictions on fans through further unjustified bans.
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz

CY2026-11-06#
party document Public statement

Ακάμας: Πόσες έρευνες χρειάζονται για να αποδοθούν επιτέλους ευθύνες;

AKEL's Head of Sustainable Development and Sustainability Sector, Christina Nicolaou, issued a written statement demanding accountability for environmental violations in the Akamas area following an Audit Service report, calling for all investigations to be submitted, full financial and environmental accounting to be published, and for the government and ministry to be held responsible.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Τα ευρήματα της Ελεγκτικής Υπηρεσίας (ΕΥ) για τον Ακάμα είναι καταπέλτης. Επιβεβαιώνουν με τον πιο επίσημο τρόπο όσα το ΑΚΕΛ καταγγέλλει από το 2023: παραβιάσεις περιβαλλοντικών όρων, ανεπαρκείς έλεγχοι, σπατάλη δημόσιων πόρων και απουσία ουσιαστικής λογοδοσίας.
The findings of the Audit Service regarding Akamas are damning. They confirm in the most official way what AKEL has been denouncing since 2023: violations of environmental terms, inadequate controls, waste of public resources and absence of substantial accountability.
Χριστίνα Νικολάου
Το ΑΚΕΛ απαιτεί να κατατεθούν όλες οι έρευνες, να δημοσιοποιηθεί πλήρης οικονομικός και περιβαλλοντικός απολογισμός και να δοθούν σαφείς απαντήσεις για τις παραλείψεις και τις αποφάσεις που οδήγησαν στη σημερινή κατάσταση.
AKEL demands that all investigations be submitted, that a full financial and environmental accounting be published, and that clear answers be given for the omissions and decisions that led to the current situation.
AKEL

Καθολική, συνεχής και συνεπής η αλληλεγγύη της διεθνούς Αριστεράς προς την Κύπρο

AKEL General Secretary Stefanos Stefanou hosted over 30 left-wing parties from around the world for AKEL's 100th anniversary and an international symposium on "The Left in the Modern World," briefing them on the Cyprus issue and receiving expressions of solidarity for a solution based on international law and UN resolutions.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Έχουμε τη χαρά να φιλοξενούμε στην Κύπρο πάνω από 30 κόμματα της Αριστεράς από ολόκληρο τον κόσμο τα οποία έχουμε προσκαλέσει για να τιμήσουμε μαζί τα 100 χρόνια ζωής του ΑΚΕΛ αλλά και την ίδια ώρα για να συμμετέχουν στο Διεθνές Συμπόσιο που οργανώνουμε με θέμα «Η Αριστερά στο σύγχρονο κόσμο».
We have the pleasure of hosting in Cyprus over 30 left-wing parties from around the world which we have invited to honor together the 100 years of AKEL's life and at the same time to participate in the International Symposium we are organizing on the theme 'The Left in the Modern World.'
Stefanos Stefanou
Πρέπει να πω ότι η αλληλεγγύη και υποστήριξη των κομμάτων της Αριστεράς για την Κύπρο, για τη λύση του Κυπριακού, είναι καθολική, είναι συνεπής και συνεχής.
I must say that the solidarity and support of the left-wing parties for Cyprus, for the solution of the Cyprus issue, is universal, it is consistent and continuous.
Stefanos Stefanou

DE2026-11-06#
party document Public statement

Tino Chrupalla: Bundesregierung braucht Realismus statt Luftschlösser

AfD federal spokesman Tino Chrupalla criticized the German government's economic optimism and called for immediate spending cuts, tax reductions, and elimination of inefficiencies in social insurance systems in response to the federal government needing up to nine billion euros to support unemployment insurance.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Am Wirtschaftsstandort Deutschland brennt es lichterloh. Doch statt die Realität endlich anzuerkennen, verbreitet die Bundesregierung weiterhin unberechtigten Optimismus und baut Luftschlösser – ob nun mit Blick auf die wirtschaftlichen Chancen dank KI, die Investitionsbereitschaft internationaler Investoren, die Klimawende oder die Perspektiven der Ukraine.
Germany as a business location is burning fiercely. But instead of finally acknowledging reality, the federal government continues to spread unjustified optimism and builds castles in the air – whether regarding economic opportunities thanks to AI, the investment readiness of international investors, the climate transition, or the prospects of Ukraine.
Tino Chrupalla
Wir müssen in Deutschland sofort alle verzichtbaren Ausgaben stoppen. Dann können die Steuern sinken und erst dann lohnen sich Arbeit und Investitionen wieder.
We must immediately stop all dispensable spending in Germany. Then taxes can be reduced and only then will work and investments be worthwhile again.
Tino Chrupalla

Alice Weidel: Die politisch gewollte Deindustrialisierung schreitet immer schneller voran

AfD federal spokesperson Alice Weidel criticized the Merz government's policies following warnings from the Gesamtmetall employers' association about 300,000 additional industrial job losses, calling for abolition of CO2 pricing and an end to climate protection policies and decarbonization plans.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die Warnung des Gesamtmetall-Verbandes, 300.000 weitere Jobs seien durch die Deindustrialisierung den kommenden zwei bis drei Jahren gefährdet, müssen Merz und seine Regierung endlich aufwecken, dass es so nicht weitergehen kann.
The warning from the Gesamtmetall association that 300,000 additional jobs are threatened by deindustrialization in the coming two to three years must finally wake up Merz and his government that things cannot continue like this.
Alice Weidel
Die CO2-Bepreisung gehört ersatzlos abgeschafft! Wir sagen: macht Schluss mit der ‚Klimaschutzpolitik' und mit den Plänen zur Dekarbonisierung und ‚Transformation der Gesellschaft'.
CO2 pricing must be abolished without replacement! We say: end 'climate protection policy' and the plans for decarbonization and 'transformation of society'.
Alice Weidel

Sven Tritschler: CDU-Bildungsministerin will KI-Steuer statt KI-Innovation

AfD deputy federal spokesperson Sven Tritschler criticized CDU Education Minister Karin Prien's proposal for a new tax on corporate profits from AI, arguing it would deter investment and contradict Germany's goal of becoming an AI nation by 2030.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Es wäre zum Lachen, wenn es nicht zum Weinen wäre: Da entwickelt sich die Weltwirtschaft beim Thema KI rasant weiter, während Deutschland den Anschluss verliert. Doch statt endlich die zentralen Innovations-Hemmnisse wie die überbordende Bürokratie zu beseitigen, denkt man in Teilen der CDU bereits laut über die nächste Steuer nach.
It would be laughable if it weren't so sad: The global economy is rapidly advancing on the topic of AI, while Germany is losing touch. But instead of finally eliminating the central innovation obstacles like excessive bureaucracy, parts of the CDU are already thinking out loud about the next tax.
Sven Tritschler
Denn zusätzliche Steuern und Abgaben schrecken Investoren und kreative Köpfe ab.
Because additional taxes and levies deter investors and creative minds.
Sven Tritschler

DE2026-11-02#
party document Public statement

Alice Weidel: Steuern nachhaltig senken statt regulatorische Preisdeckel

AfD federal spokesperson Alice Weidel criticized the government's planned fuel price cap, calling instead for sustainable tax cuts including reduced VAT on fuel and elimination of CO2 taxation.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die Bundesregierung und Ministerin Reiche verfolgen den völlig falschen Ansatz, indem sie den Spritpreis künstlich zu deckeln versuchen. So viel Markt wie möglich, so viel Staat wie nötig ist das Grundprinzip unserer Sozialen Marktwirtschaft. Und dabei sollte es auch bleiben.
The federal government and Minister Reiche are pursuing a completely wrong approach by trying to artificially cap fuel prices. As much market as possible, as much state as necessary is the basic principle of our social market economy. And it should remain that way.
Alice Weidel
Der viel zu hohe Steueranteil ist die Hauptursache für die hohen Kraftstoff- und Energiepreise in Deutschland. Statt bürokratischer Steuergeld-Umverteilung brauchen die Bürger jetzt echte Entlastung durch deutliche Steuersenkungen. Die Mehrwertsteuer auf Kraftstoffe muss runter, die CO2-Besteuerung muss weg.
The far too high tax share is the main cause of high fuel and energy prices in Germany. Instead of bureaucratic tax money redistribution, citizens now need real relief through significant tax cuts. VAT on fuel must come down, CO2 taxation must go.
Alice Weidel

Verteilungsbericht: Weckruf für die Vermögensteuer

Die Linke party chair Luigi Pantisano responded to the Hans-Böckler-Stiftung distribution report by calling for a wealth tax on multimillionaires and billionaires, criticizing the Merz government for refusing this debate while the lower middle class shrinks and nearly one in six households lives in poverty.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Wer in diesem Land arbeitet, muss davon leben können, und das muss auch am Ende des Monats noch gelten.
Anyone who works in this country must be able to live from it, and that must still apply at the end of the month.
Luigi Pantisano
Fast jeder sechste Haushalt ist arm, die untere Mittelschicht schrumpft und gleichzeitig werden Reiche immer reicher. Das ist kein Naturgesetz, sondern das Ergebnis politischer Entscheidungen dieser Bundesregierung.
Almost one in six households is poor, the lower middle class is shrinking and at the same time the rich are getting richer. This is not a law of nature, but the result of political decisions by this federal government.
Luigi Pantisano

Mai Kivelä: Hallituksen lakiesitys lyhytvuokrauksen ongelmiin puuttumiseksi jäi täysin hampaattomaksi

Left Alliance MP Mai Kivelä submitted a dissenting opinion in the Environment Committee against the government's proposal on short-term rental regulation, arguing it weakens current law by cementing a 90-day rental right and provides no tools for municipalities to address problems.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Hallituksen esitys jätti alun alkaenkin toivomisen varaa, mutta valiokuntakäsittelyssä se lässähti totaalisesti
The government's proposal left much to be desired from the start, but in committee processing it completely fell flat
Mai Kivelä
Käytännössä lakiesitys nyt heikentää nykytilaa sementoimalla lakiin oikeuden lyhytvuokrata asuntoa joka tapauksessa 90 päivän ajan
In practice, the legislative proposal now weakens the current situation by cementing into law the right to short-term rent an apartment for 90 days in any case
Mai Kivelä

FI2026-11-05#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Honkasalo vaatii Helsingin tekoälyn käytölle selkeitä pelisääntöjä valtuustoaloitteella

Left Alliance city councillor and MP Veronika Honkasalo submitted a council initiative on 4 November 2026 demanding that the City of Helsinki investigate how artificial intelligence is used across municipal services and establish clear, democratically processed principles for AI use, including safeguards for data protection, equality, transparency, and citizens' rights to human decision-making.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Meidän on tiedettävä, mitä tekoälyllä kaupungin palveluissa tehdään ja millaisia vaikutuksia sillä on ihmisten arkeen. Erityisesti lasten, nuorten ja haavoittuvassa asemassa olevien asiakkaiden kohdalla tarvitaan selkeitä rajoja. Periaatteellista päätöstä tekoälyn hyödyntämisestä ei pidä jättää yksin virkamiesjohdon käsiin, vaan myös luottamushenkilöiden ja kaupunkilaisten on voitava osallistua yhteisten pelisääntöjen määrittelyyn
We must know what is being done with AI in the city's services and what effects it has on people's everyday lives. Especially in the case of children, young people and clients in vulnerable positions, clear boundaries are needed. The principled decision on the use of AI should not be left solely to the civil service management, but elected officials and residents must also be able to participate in defining common rules
Veronika Honkasalo
Tekoäly voi olla hyödyllinen työkalu, mutta vastuun päätöksistä on säilyttävä ihmisellä. Kaupunkilaisilla on oltava oikeus tietää, milloin heidän asioidensa käsittelyssä käytetään tekoälyä
AI can be a useful tool, but responsibility for decisions must remain with humans. Residents must have the right to know when AI is used in processing their matters
Veronika Honkasalo

Pia Lohikoski: Vasemmistoliitto ei kannata tasokokeita perusopetukseen

Left Alliance MP Pia Lohikoski opposes the Audit Committee's proposal for mandatory national standardized tests in basic education, citing unanimous opposition from the Education Committee and over 650 education researchers.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Teimme asiasta sivistysvaliokunnassa yksimielisen lausunnon, jossa kansallisia kokeita ei kannateta. Nyt myös yli 650 kasvatuksen ja koulutuksen tutkijaa on allekirjoittanut tasokokeita vastustavan vetoomuksen

Pia Lohikoski

A significant risk in national standardized tests is also that schools will start to be ranked based on them, and segregation will only increase.

FI2026-11-04#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Merja Kyllönen ja Katri Kulmuni: AdBlue-ongelman ratkaisu eteni EU-parlamentissa

Left Alliance MEP Merja Kyllönen and Centre Party MEP Katri Kulmuni secured European Parliament committee support for provisions addressing freezing problems in diesel vehicle AdBlue emission-control systems in cold northern conditions, as part of the Automotive Omnibus regulatory package.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Suomessa pakkanen kuuluu tavalliseen talveen. Kuljetusyrittäjän tai autoilijan ei pitäisi joutua maksamaan siitä, etteivät ajoneuvon järjestelmät kestä niitä oloja, joissa niitä käytetään. Olemme vaatineet tähän korjausta, ja nyt työ tuottaa tulosta
In Finland, freezing temperatures are part of a normal winter. A transport entrepreneur or motorist should not have to pay for the fact that vehicle systems cannot withstand the conditions in which they are used. We have demanded a correction to this, and now the work is producing results
Merja Kyllönen
Pohjoisen ongelman saaminen mukaan laajaan eurooppalaiseen lakipakettiin ei ole ollut itsestään selvää. Suomalainen sisu on tullut tarpeeseen, mutta ennen kaikkea tulos on syntynyt yhteistyöstä yli puoluerajojen ja alan toimijoilta saadusta tuesta
Getting the northern problem included in a broad European legislative package has not been self-evident. Finnish determination has been needed, but above all the result has come from cooperation across party lines and support from industry actors
Katri Kulmuni

FI2026-11-03#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Timo Furuholm: Keinoja köyhyyskriisin ratkaisemiseksi kyllä löytyisi

Left Alliance MP Timo Furuholm delivered a group speech in parliament during debate on an opposition interpellation concerning the cost of living, criticizing the right-wing government for worsening poverty and proposing measures including restoring social security protections, child supplements to unemployment benefits, full index increases to benefits, restarting social housing production, and establishing a national crisis mechanism to support low-income people.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Ministeri Purra lupasi, että pienituloisilta leikkaaminen ei perussuomalaisille käy. Sen jälkeen pienituloisilta on leikattu jokaisessa budjetissa. Ei tullut euron bensaa. Ei tullut 100 000 uutta työpaikkaa. Ei lopetettu velaksi elämistä. Sen sijaan saatiin työttömyys- ja köyhyyskriisi.
Minister Purra promised that cutting from low-income people would not be acceptable to the Finns Party. After that, low-income people have been cut in every budget. There was no one-euro gasoline. There were no 100,000 new jobs. Living on debt was not stopped. Instead, we got an unemployment and poverty crisis.
Timo Furuholm
Vasemmistoliitto palauttaisi suojaosat sosiaaliturvaan, lapsikorotuksen työttömyysturvaan ja toteuttaisi indeksikorotukset etuuksiin täysimääräisesti. Myös alas ajettu sosiaalinen asuntotuotanto pitäisi potkaista jälleen käyntiin, jotta ihmisillä olisi tulevaisuudessakin kohtuuhintaisia koteja.
The Left Alliance would restore protected portions to social security, child supplements to unemployment benefits, and implement index increases to benefits in full. The run-down social housing production should also be kicked back into action so that people would have affordable homes in the future as well.
Timo Furuholm

La France doit protéger Julide Yazici du régime Erdogan

The France Unbowed parliamentary group demands the French government publicly intervene to protect Jülide Yazici, a Turkish Marxist activist and political refugee in France since 2021, from extradition by Albania to Turkey where she faces persecution under the Erdogan regime.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

France, by recognizing Jülide Yazici as a political refugee, has also established that she is subject to political persecution.
France Unbowed parliamentary group
Nous exigeons une prise de position publique du gouvernement sur la protection accordée par la France à cette militante.
We demand a public position from the government on the protection granted by France to this activist.
France Unbowed parliamentary group

FR2026-11-04#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Nouveau nucléaire : macronistes, droite, extrême droite et socialistes valident le chèque en blanc au détriment du logement social

France Unbowed's parliamentary group opposed in the Finance Committee an article financing the new French nuclear program of six EPR2 reactors estimated at 250 billion euros, arguing it would divert 60 billion euros from the Livret A savings account away from social housing, and pledged to cancel the nuclear program if Jean-Luc Mélenchon is elected in 2027.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Tant pis pour nos concitoyens des classes populaires : macronistes, droite, extrême-droite et socialistes ont choisi de valider le chèque en blanc au nouveau nucléaire au détriment du logement social.
Too bad for our working-class citizens: Macronists, the right, the far-right and socialists have chosen to validate the blank check for new nuclear at the expense of social housing.
France Unbowed parliamentary group
En 2027, avec l'élection de Jean-Luc Mélenchon, nous annulerons cette relance du nucléaire et lancerons une politique de bifurcation énergétique appuyée sur la sobriété, l'efficacité énergétique et le développement des énergies renouvelables.
In 2027, with the election of Jean-Luc Mélenchon, we will cancel this nuclear revival and launch an energy transition policy based on sobriety, energy efficiency and the development of renewable energies.
France Unbowed parliamentary group

The France Unbowed parliamentary group supports a citizen petition with over 500,000 signatures calling for CNews to be removed from French broadcasting, demanding the National Assembly debate the issue and take appropriate measures following a parliamentary inquiry led by deputy Aurélien Saintoul in May 2024.

France Unbowed parliamentary group

France Unbowed parliamentary group

party document Public statement

Sauver Asalée !

The France Unbowed parliamentary group issued a statement supporting the cooperative takeover of Asalée, a healthcare association facing judicial liquidation, by nurses, doctors, patients, and public authorities rather than private investors.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Asalée appartient à la collectivité, pas à un investisseur !
Asalée belongs to the community, not to an investor!
France Unbowed parliamentary group

To de-financialize healthcare, to democratize care, to restore meaning to work, we therefore give our total support to the cooperative takeover project.
France Unbowed parliamentary group

The New Anticapitalist Party – Revolutionaries criticizes Trump and Robert Francis Kennedy Jr.'s anti-vaccination policy, stating it has caused thousands of measles cases in the United States, reaching levels unseen in thirty-five years.

As a direct consequence of the anti-vaccination policy advocated by Trump and his Health Minister, Robert Francis Kennedy Jr., thousands of measles cases have been recorded since the beginning of the year, a level unprecedented in thirty-five years.

GB2026-11-06#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Dr Ellie Chowns urges PM to publish a roadmap for phasing out fossil fuel company tax breaks at COP31

Green Party Parliamentary leader Dr Ellie Chowns wrote to the Prime Minister urging publication of a roadmap to phase out fossil fuel company tax breaks at COP31, strengthen the Oil and Gas Revenue Levy, and push for a global profit tax on oil companies through the UN Tax Convention.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Less than ten years ago the UK took pride in being a climate leader on the global stage, with overwhelming cross-party consensus on the need for urgent action to reduce emissions and transition away from climate-wrecking fossil fuels. How quickly we have fallen.
Dr Ellie Chowns
Against the political backdrop of the Tories abandoning their commitment to net zero and Reform UK adopting outright climate-denialism, it is unforgiveable that Andy Burnham's Labour is also now taking shocking steps backward when it comes to our climate commitments: approving Jackdaw, refusing to rule out greenlighting Rosebank oilfield, and cutting funding for vital home insultation and clean heating programmes.
Dr Ellie Chowns

Green Party responds to continuation of power-sharing in Sheffield

The Green Party of England and Wales issued a statement after Sheffield City Council's Labour Group reversed its decision to end power-sharing with the Green Group, criticizing Labour leadership's attempted intervention and linking it to Labour's position on Israel.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
In Sheffield, the Green Group have stood firmly by their principles, and are continuing to put residents above political point-scoring. The local Labour Party have also realised that smashing apart their administration at the whims of Labour HQ isn't in residents' interests.
Green Party spokesperson
Andy Burnham's authoritarian diktat has angered councillors and residents across the country, including those in his own party. The Labour Party leadership's attempts to force local councillors to tear up agreements they've worked hard to maintain have resulted in their own councillors defying this overreach.
Green Party spokesperson

GB2026-11-04#
party document Public statement

Green Party Response to Drug-Related Death Figures

The Green Party of England and Wales issued a statement criticizing the government for lacking a dedicated drugs minister and called for immediate evidence-based drug policy measures including Overdose Prevention Centres, Heroin Assisted Treatment, increased Naloxone provision, drug checking services, and improved prison harm reduction services in response to 5,519 drug poisoning deaths in 2025.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Our call to action is to contact your MP now, today, and ask them to act on behalf of people who have little or no voice, to follow the evidence, and make life-saving changes to their policy of inaction and avoidance. Please ask your MP to support the opening of OPCs in their constituencies. Let's hold the government to account and see the number of unnecessary deaths reduce for the first time in over a decade.
Vicki Beere

GB2026-11-02#
party document Public statement

Joint statement from The Green Party and the Local Government Association Green Group

The Green Party and its Local Government Association Green Group issued a joint statement criticizing national parties for forcing local council leaders to end progressive partnerships with Labour, Independent, and Liberal Democrat councillors, defending their commitment to local power-sharing agreements and democratic decision-making.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
The Green Party, and the Green Group Office, representing Green councillors across the country, have been hugely disappointed in moves designed by national parties to force local council leaders into jeopardising successful progressive partnerships that are delivering benefits for residents.
Green Party and Green Group Office
Partnership is at the heart of Green politics. We believe in working together for the good of people and planet, even when that means working alongside people and parties with whom we disagree on some national and international issues.
Green Party and Green Group Office

GR2026-11-05#
party document Public statement

ÎšĎ…ÎşÎťÎŹÎ´ÎľĎ‚: Î— Ď‡ÎľÎšÎźÎľĎÎšÎ˝ÎŽ ÎąÎşĎ„ÎżĎ€ÎťÎżÎÎą ĎƒĎ„Îą Ď‡Î­ĎÎšÎą Ď„Ď‰Î˝ ÎşÎżÎšÎ˝Ď‰Î˝ÎšĎŽÎ˝ ÎşÎąÎš ĎŒĎ‡Îš Ď„Ď‰Î˝ ÎľĎ†ÎżĎ€ÎťÎšĎƒĎ„ĎŽÎ˝

MeRA25 criticizes the government's winter ferry schedule for the Cyclades islands, calling it inadequate and prioritizing shipowners' profits over local communities' needs, and demands immediate revision with public control and social criteria.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Ο χειμερινός ακτοπλοϊκός σχεδιασμός για τη σύνδεση των Κυκλάδων με τον Πειραιά είναι ενδεικτικός των προτεραιοτήτων της κυβέρνησης: ασυδοσία και κέρδη για τους εφοπλιστές, την ώρα που οι τοπικές κοινωνίες εγκαταλείπονται στη μοίρα τους.
The winter ferry schedule for connecting the Cyclades with Piraeus is indicative of the government's priorities: impunity and profits for shipowners, while local communities are abandoned to their fate.
MeRA25
Το ΜέΡΑ25 Ενωτική Αριστερά απαιτεί την άμεση αναθεώρηση των χειμερινών δρομολογίων, καθημερινές ενδοκυκλαδικές συνδέσεις, ουσιαστική μείωση των εισιτηρίων και εξασφαλισμένη μεταφορά των ασθενών.
MeRA25 United Left demands the immediate revision of winter routes, daily inter-Cycladic connections, substantial reduction of ticket prices, and guaranteed transport for patients.
MeRA25

GR2026-11-08#
party document Policy proposal

Ρ. Δούρου: Ο κοινοβουλευτικός έλεγχος που ασκούμε προς την κυβέρνηση έρχεται από την κοινωνία για την κοινωνία

SYRIZA-PS Parliamentary Group President Rena Dourou stated that the party's parliamentary oversight comes from civil society, criticized the government's handling of local government autonomy and funding, and announced the party has secured its parliamentary entry while claiming to have gathered 119 votes for a Vice President proposal.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Δεν βρισκόμαστε στο νομό Κορινθίας για πρώτη φορά, ούτε γιατί πλησιάζουν εκλογές. Οι αγωνίστριες και αγωνιστές του ΣΥΡΙΖΑ-ΠΣ βρίσκονται εκεί όπου πρέπει. Όχι πέριξ του Συντάγματος με διάφορους τεχνοκράτες συμβούλους.
We are not in the prefecture of Corinth for the first time, nor because elections are approaching. The fighters of SYRIZA-PS are where they should be. Not around Syntagma with various technocrat advisors.
Rena Dourou
Ο κοινοβουλευτικός έλεγχος που ασκούμε εμείς προς την κυβέρνηση έρχεται από τον Δικηγορικό Σύλλογο, από τους εργαζόμενους στο Εργατικό Κέντρο. Έρχεται δηλαδή από την κοινωνία για την κοινωνία.
The parliamentary oversight that we exercise towards the government comes from the Bar Association, from the workers at the Labor Center. It comes from society for society.
Rena Dourou

GR2026-11-07#
party document Policy proposal

Ν. Παππάς: «2,5 δισ. ευρώ παραπάνω ΦΠΑ μέχρι τον Σεπτέμβριο. Να επιστρέψει η κυβέρνηση μέρισμα στην κοινωνία με μηδενικό ΦΠΑ στα βασικά αγαθά για έξι μήνες»

SYRIZA-PS Secretary Nikos Pappas proposed zero VAT on basic goods for six months, citing 2.5 billion euros in additional VAT revenue collected through September 2026, and called on the government to return a dividend to society.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Τα στοιχεία δείχνουν ότι το κράτος εισπράττει πολύ περισσότερα από τη φορολογία, ενώ η κυβέρνηση προβλέπει πρωτογενή πλεονάσματα σημαντικά υψηλότερα από τους αρχικούς της στόχους. Μόνο μέχρι τον Σεπτέμβριο εισέπραξε 2,5 δισ. ευρώ περισσότερο ΦΠΑ από πέρυσι.
The data show that the state is collecting much more from taxation, while the government forecasts primary surpluses significantly higher than its initial targets. Only until September it collected 2.5 billion euros more VAT than last year.
Nikos Pappas
Προτείνουμε μηδενισμό του ΦΠΑ για έξι μήνες σε επιλεγμένα βασικά τρόφιμα, φάρμακα και βρεφικά είδη, με δυνατότητα παράτασης για ακόμη έξι μήνες.
We propose zero VAT for six months on selected basic foods, medicines and baby products, with the possibility of extension for another six months.
Nikos Pappas

party document Public statement

Ρ. Δούρου: Πώς θα αποτρέψει η κυβέρνηση την επανάληψη της περίπτωσης της Κάσου;

SYRIZA-PS parliamentary group president Rena Dourou questioned how the government will prevent a repeat of the Kasos incident, where Turkish vessels blocked cable-laying in violation of Greek sovereign rights, criticizing the government's lack of a coherent national strategy.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Το ερώτημα που πρέπει να απαντήσει η κυβέρνηση είναι το πώς θα αποτρέψει την επανάληψη της περίπτωσης της Κάσου. Όταν δηλαδή τουρκικά πλοία εμπόδισαν την πόντιση, παραβιάζοντας κυριαρχικά δικαιώματα της Ελλάδας.
The question the government must answer is how it will prevent a repeat of the Kasos case. When Turkish ships blocked the laying, violating Greece's sovereign rights.
Rena Dourou
Ο ΣΥΡΙΖΑ-ΠΣ επιμένει σταθερά στον διάλογο και τους ανοικτούς διαύλους επικοινωνίας με την Τουρκία, με σαφείς κόκκινες γραμμές, στο πλαίσιο συνεκτικής εθνικής στρατηγικής – αυτήν ακριβώς που εδώ και επτά χρόνια δεν διαθέτει η ελληνική κυβέρνηση με τις ανάλογες συνέπειες…
SYRIZA-PS insists firmly on dialogue and open channels of communication with Turkey, with clear red lines, within the framework of a coherent national strategy – precisely what the Greek government has not had for seven years with the corresponding consequences...
Rena Dourou

Τόσο καιρό, όταν ζητούσαμε μειώσεις στον ΦΠΑ, η κυβέρνηση μας έλεγε ότι δεν θα φτάσουν στον καταναλωτή, γιατί θα τις απορροφήσει η αγορά.
For so long, when we were asking for VAT reductions, the government told us they would not reach the consumer, because the market would absorb them.
Afroditi Latinopoulou
Όταν τη μείωση του ΦΠΑ τη ζητούσε η αντιπολίτευση, ήταν «λαϊκισμός». Όταν την εξαγγέλλει ο κ. Μητσοτάκης με ορίζοντα τις εκλογές, γίνεται κοινωνική ευαισθησία.
When the opposition was asking for the VAT reduction, it was 'populism'. When Mr. Mitsotakis announces it with elections on the horizon, it becomes social sensitivity.
Afroditi Latinopoulou

Richard Boyd Barrett Slams Government for Refusing Public Sector Workers Cost-of-Living Pay Rises While Upping Weapon Spending

Richard Boyd Barrett TD criticised the Irish Government for refusing public sector workers cost-of-living pay rises while increasing military spending to €1.7 billion by 2030, and announced People Before Profit support for a public sector strike and Irish Anti-War Movement demonstration both scheduled for 12 November 2026.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
It is appropriate that workers will be striking for cost-of-living pay increases on the same day that anti-war protestors are mobilising against the hundreds of billions of Euros being spent on weapons of death and destruction. The Irish Government and the EU can find the money for vast increases in military spending but not to protect workers incomes. There is a clear connection between these two things.
Richard Boyd Barrett
The Irish Government has said it will increase spending on weapons and military equipment to €1.7 billion by 2030, a 55% increase on its previous military spending plans. But the same Government won't give workers a pay increase to compensate for inflation. This demonstrates this Government's lethal priorities are fully aligned to the EU's militarisation and war agenda.
Richard Boyd Barrett

Richard Boyd Barrett Calls on Taoiseach to Use Shared Ireland Funds to Save Services in Newry’s Daisy Hill Hospital

Richard Boyd Barrett raised concerns in the Dáil about the collapse of maternity services at Newry's Daisy Hill Hospital due to consultant shortages and called on the Taoiseach to use Shared Ireland funds to develop an all-Ireland health service to retain services at the hospital.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
I was in Newry on Monday night meeting campaigners who are part of the SOS Daisy Hill campaign. This campaign is opposing relocation of maternity services to Craigavon hospital because of a lack of consultants at Daisy Hill Thousands of people have marched in Newry to keep the services in Daisy Hill.
Richard Boyd Barrett
The Taoiseach should engage with this very serious issue and use the Shared Island fund in his own department to assist in developing an all-Ireland health service which will keep maternity services and other services in Daisy Hill Hospital in Newry.
Richard Boyd Barrett

Conor Reddy Calls for Local Opposition to Finglas Gambling Arcade's Bid to Extend Opening Hours to Midnight

People Before Profit Councillor Conor Reddy called on Finglas residents and organisations to oppose a planning application by Leeside Leisure Centre Ltd to extend a gambling arcade's opening hours from 10pm to midnight, arguing it threatens community wellbeing and contradicts efforts to revitalise the village.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Last year, many of us warned that allowing this gambling arcade to open could be the start of a slippery slope. We've seen it elsewhere in Dublin, where gambling arcades have opened with restricted hours and then sought to extend them once established. Unfortunately, that's exactly what's happening here.
Conor Reddy
Gambling arcades are predatory businesses. They make their money from people losing theirs, often people who are already struggling with addiction, debt and financial hardship. Extending the opening hours means two more hours of access to high-frequency gambling every night of the week, with all the potential harm that brings.
Conor Reddy

Miriam Boush is being co-opted to Carlow County Council as a People Before Profit councillor to replace Adrienne Wallace during maternity leave, becoming the first person of colour on the council, with stated priorities including banning new data centres, opposing Triple Lock changes, challenging council rent increases, and improving council transparency.

I'm delighted that Miriam will be co-opted onto the council during my maternity leave. She has a deep commitment to the people of her home county of Carlow and I know her sense of social justice, her energy and her drive will serve them well.
Adrienne Wallace
I join the council just as the Government is trying to end the Triple Lock so it can send young people abroad to fight in imperialist wars. If they get away with ending the Triple Lock, sending Irish soldiers to Lebanon on the same side as Israel against Hezbollah will just be the start.
Miriam Boush

Statement on the suspension of Workers’ Party TCD committee members

The Workers' Party of Ireland suspended the Workers' Party TCD committee pending disciplinary proceedings after members publicly burned membership cards, accused the party of being a Russian puppet, and supported NATO's role in Ukraine.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated

IT2026-11-05#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Diritti: tutela disabilità entra con più forza in costituzione

Brothers of Italy senators announced the Senate's unanimous first-reading approval of the Guidi bill amending Article 38 of the Italian Constitution to replace the term 'minorati' with 'persone con disabilità' and guarantee rights to autonomy and participation for people with disabilities.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con il ddl Guidi, approvato oggi in prima lettura al Senato con voto unanime, compiamo un passo importante verso il riconoscimento dei diritti delle persone con disabilità nella nostra Costituzione.
With the Guidi bill, approved today in first reading in the Senate with a unanimous vote, we take an important step toward the recognition of the rights of people with disabilities in our Constitution.
Andrea De Priamo
Superiamo il termine 'minorati', ormai anacronistico e connotato negativamente, per riconoscere il diritto all'autonomia e alla partecipazione effettiva alla vita politica, economica e sociale del Paese.
We move beyond the term 'minorati', now anachronistic and negatively connoted, to recognize the right to autonomy and effective participation in the political, economic and social life of the country.
Andrea De Priamo

4 novembre: omaggio a chi serve e difende Italia ogni giorno

Prime Minister Giorgia Meloni issued a statement on November 4th honoring Italy's Armed Forces and commemorating fallen soldiers, including four military personnel recently killed in Bosnia.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Il 4 novembre è il giorno in cui l'Italia rende onore alla sua storia, alla sua Unità e a tutti coloro che hanno scelto di servire la Patria, anche a costo della propria vita.
November 4th is the day when Italy honors its history, its Unity and all those who have chosen to serve the Homeland, even at the cost of their own lives.
Giorgia Meloni
In questi giorni, il dolore per la perdita dei nostri quattro militari in Bosnia ci ha ricordato, ancora una volta, quanto possa essere alto il prezzo di questa scelta.
In these days, the pain for the loss of our four military personnel in Bosnia has reminded us, once again, how high the price of this choice can be.
Giorgia Meloni

party document Policy proposal

Consumi: al via carta Dedicata a Te, aiuti per un milione di famiglie

Minister Francesco Lollobrigida announced the launch on November 4 of the "Carta Dedicata a Te" program, allocating over 1 billion euros to support one million vulnerable Italian families while supporting domestic agri-food sectors.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con l'avvio oggi 4 novembre della Carta Dedicata a Te manteniamo un impegno preciso nei confronti delle famiglie italiane, destinando oltre 1 miliardo di euro ai nuclei più fragili.
With the launch today, November 4, of the Carta Dedicata a Te, we maintain a precise commitment to Italian families, allocating over 1 billion euros to the most fragile households.
Francesco Lollobrigida
La nostra è una scelta di visione: no all'assistenzialismo passivo, sì a un sostegno concreto e dignitoso che protegge il potere d'acquisto degli italiani e, al contempo, difende l'eccellenza delle nostre filiere agroalimentari, della pesca e dell'agricoltura di qualità.
Ours is a choice of vision: no to passive welfare, yes to concrete and dignified support that protects the purchasing power of Italians and, at the same time, defends the excellence of our agri-food supply chains, fishing and quality agriculture.
Francesco Lollobrigida

IT2026-11-04#
party document Policy proposal

Agricoltura: oltre un miliardo per rafforzare filiere italiane

The Italian Senate unanimously approved the ColtivaItalia bill, allocating over 1.1 billion euros to support Italian agriculture through business support, research, generational renewal, and Made in Italy protection.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con l'approvazione definitiva del ddl ColtivaItalia, diventa legge un importante provvedimento a sostegno dei nostri agricoltori. Oltre un miliardo di euro di investimenti per sostenere chi ogni giorno, con sacrificio, passione e dedizione, lavora la nostra terra, custodisce le nostre tradizioni e contribuisce alla crescita dell'economia nazionale.
With the final approval of the ColtivaItalia bill, an important measure in support of our farmers becomes law. Over one billion euros of investments to support those who every day, with sacrifice, passion and dedication, work our land, preserve our traditions and contribute to the growth of the national economy.
Giorgia Meloni
Oggi il Senato della Repubblica ha approvato senza nessun voto contrario il Coltivaitalia, una proposta che avevo presentato nel 2025: oltre 1 miliardo e 100 milioni di euro per interventi destinati a rafforzare il mondo agricolo italiano, renderlo più forte in termini strutturali, intervenire sulle criticità e aiutare l'Italia a essere garantita nelle sue produzioni di alta qualità e poterle mettere a disposizione dei suoi cittadini e del mondo.
Today the Senate of the Republic approved without any vote against ColtivaItalia, a proposal I presented in 2025: over 1 billion and 100 million euros for interventions aimed at strengthening the Italian agricultural world, making it structurally stronger, addressing critical issues and helping Italy to be guaranteed in its high-quality productions and to make them available to its citizens and the world.
Francesco Lollobrigida

Sud: ‘Locomotiva d’Italia’, un tour per illustrare successi governo

Brothers of Italy launches a multi-city tour across southern Italy called 'Sud - Locomotiva d'Italia' to illustrate the Meloni government's achievements for the Mezzogiorno, including the Zes Unica special economic zone and employment measures.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Questo Governo considera tra le sue priorità il Sud. E sono tante le misure, a partire dalla Zes Unica, che ne dimostrano l'impegno. I risultati, a differenza di quanto asserisce la sinistra, sono i fatti: pil in aumento, occupazione record, soprattutto al Sud e soprattutto tra giovani e donne.
This Government considers the South among its priorities. And there are many measures, starting with the Zes Unica, that demonstrate the commitment. The results, unlike what the left asserts, are the facts: GDP increasing, record employment, especially in the South and especially among young people and women.
Galeazzo Bignami
Con Fratelli d'Italia, non più assistenzialismo ma lavoro: il Mezzogiorno è diventato la locomotiva della Nazione per crescita e occupazione. E adesso grazie alla manovra del governo Meloni raddoppiamo i fondi per la Zes unica e proroghiamo gli sgravi al 100% per chi assume.
With Brothers of Italy, no more welfare dependency but work: the Mezzogiorno has become the locomotive of the Nation for growth and employment. And now thanks to the Meloni government's budget we are doubling the funds for the Zes Unica and extending 100% tax breaks for those who hire.
Lucio Malan

IT2026-11-03#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Covid: governo Conte rifiutò monoclonali gratuiti. Speranza ha mentito

Brothers of Italy parliamentarians accused the former Conte II government and ex-Health Minister Speranza of wrongly refusing a free donation of 10,000 monoclonal antibody doses from Eli Lilly during the pandemic while investing 15 million euros in a competing Tuscan foundation project that never produced doses.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Ricevere 10 mila dosi di monoclonali da parte di Eli Lilly durante la pandemia rappresentava 'un'opportunità straordinariamente interessante' per curare i pazienti affetti da Covid. Opportunità che tuttavia il governo Conte II ha inspiegabilmente e colpevolmente rifiutato.
Receiving 10 thousand doses of monoclonals from Eli Lilly during the pandemic represented 'an extraordinarily interesting opportunity' to treat patients affected by Covid. An opportunity that however the Conte II government inexplicably and culpably refused.
Alice Buonguerrieri
Il progetto, finanziato con soldi pubblici, non ha mai realmente visto produzione di una sola dose di anticorpo monoclonale, si è rivelato soltanto un vergognoso spreco di denaro pubblico, soprattutto considerando che l'Italia avrebbe potuto salvare molte vite senza spendere un euro grazie ai monoclonali della Eli Lilly
The project, financed with public money, never actually saw production of a single dose of monoclonal antibody, it proved to be only a shameful waste of public money, especially considering that Italy could have saved many lives without spending a euro thanks to Eli Lilly's monoclonals
Antonella Zedda

IT2026-11-03#
party document Public statement

Cybersicurezza: guerra ibrida è realtà, investire in difesa

Brothers of Italy parliamentarians called for increased investment in cybersecurity and defense following cyberattacks on European infrastructure including Italian supermarkets, describing hybrid warfare as a current reality and praising the Meloni government's response through the National Cybersecurity Agency.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Gli attacchi informatici che in queste ore stanno colpendo infrastrutture e attività produttive in diversi Paesi europei, Italia compresa, dimostrano quanto la sicurezza cibernetica sia ormai parte integrante della nostra sicurezza nazionale. La guerra ibrida non è uno scenario futuro: è una realtà con cui dobbiamo confrontarci già oggi.
The cyberattacks that are currently hitting infrastructure and productive activities in several European countries, including Italy, demonstrate how much cybersecurity is now an integral part of our national security. Hybrid warfare is not a future scenario: it is a reality we must confront today.
Michele Barcaiuolo
Per questo è fondamentale continuare a investire nella difesa, nella sicurezza e nella resilienza cibernetica del nostro Paese. Proteggere le infrastrutture strategiche, le imprese, i servizi essenziali e il sistema finanziario significa proteggere concretamente la libertà e la sicurezza degli italiani.
For this reason it is essential to continue investing in defense, security and cyber resilience of our country. Protecting strategic infrastructure, businesses, essential services and the financial system means concretely protecting the freedom and security of Italians.
Michele Barcaiuolo

The Italian Communist Party participated as a delegation in the centenary celebration of AKEL (Progressive Party of Working People) in Nicosia, Cyprus, on November 6-7, alongside more than thirty communist parties from around the world, and attended an international seminar on the contemporary left.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

Italian Communist Party

IT2026-11-07#
party document Public statement

A 109 anni dalla rivoluzione d’Ottobre

The Italian Communist Party issued a statement commemorating the 109th anniversary of the October Revolution, reaffirming its communist identity and calling for continued struggle for socialism against capitalism, imperialism, and inequality.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con la Rivoluzione d'Ottobre le parole Socialismo e Comunismo uscirono dal solo campo delle idee per entrare nella concretezza della realizzazione storica.
With the October Revolution the words Socialism and Communism left the field of ideas alone to enter the concreteness of historical realization.
Partito Comunista Italiano
Oggi, in un mondo attraversato da guerre, imperialismo, precarietà e ingiustizie, ricordare la Rivoluzione d'Ottobre non significa guardare nostalgicamente al passato. Significa riconoscere che la necessità di trasformare radicalmente la società è ancora tutta davanti a noi.
Today, in a world crossed by wars, imperialism, precarity and injustices, remembering the October Revolution does not mean looking nostalgically at the past. It means recognizing that the necessity to radically transform society is still entirely before us.
Partito Comunista Italiano

IT2026-11-06#
party document Public statement

PCI: rapimento e detenzione di Giorgia Lombardi e Lorenzo Diofili; la libertà non si tocca, subito il rilascio!

The Ligurian Federation of the Italian Communist Party condemned the detention of Italian volunteers Giorgia Lombardi and Lorenzo Diofili in the West Bank and called on the Italian government to secure their immediate release through diplomatic intervention.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Come Federazione ligure del Partito Comunista Italiano esprimiamo la più profonda indignazione e la ferma condanna per il rapimento e la detenzione degli spezzini Giorgia Lombardi e Lorenzo Diofili, volontari italiani impegnati in Cisgiordania al fianco della popolazione palestinese e delle comunità contadine, in un'attività di solidarietà e accompagnamento internazionale.
As the Ligurian Federation of the Italian Communist Party we express the deepest indignation and firm condemnation for the kidnapping and detention of Giorgia Lombardi and Lorenzo Diofili from La Spezia, Italian volunteers engaged in the West Bank alongside the Palestinian population and peasant communities, in an activity of solidarity and international accompaniment.
Partito Comunista Italiano - Federazione della Liguria
Rivolgiamo un appello diretto al Governo italiano, alla Presidenza del Consiglio e al Ministro degli Esteri affinché la Farnesina intervenga immediatamente, utilizzando tutti gli strumenti diplomatici a disposizione per ottenere la liberazione di Giorgia e di Lorenzo assieme alle altre volontarie e agli altri volontari coinvolti, verificare le loro condizioni, garantire la loro incolumità e assicurare il pieno rispetto dei loro diritti.
We make a direct appeal to the Italian Government, to the Presidency of the Council and to the Foreign Minister so that the Farnesina intervenes immediately, using all available diplomatic instruments to obtain the release of Giorgia and Lorenzo together with the other volunteers involved, verify their conditions, guarantee their safety and ensure full respect for their rights.
Partito Comunista Italiano - Federazione della Liguria

IT2026-11-06#
party document Public statement

La strategia dello smantellamento

The Italian Communist Party criticizes the 2027 Budget Law presented by the Meloni government, arguing it dismantles collective guarantees through selective tax measures, pension restrictions, and insufficient funding for public health and education while shifting responsibility for rights from the state to individual economic capacity.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Prima di entrare nel merito delle misure principali, voglio dire che, a monte, la filosofia che governa anche questa Manovra di bilancio è stata quella di concentrare le risorse, non moltissime, che avevamo a disposizione sulle stesse priorità alle quali ci siamo dedicati nei quattro anni che sono passati: famiglia, natalità, riduzione delle tasse, tutela del potere d'acquisto, incentivi al lavoro, incentivi alle imprese, salute dei cittadini
Before going into the details of the main measures, I want to say that, fundamentally, the philosophy governing this Budget Maneuver has been to concentrate the resources, not very many, that we had available on the same priorities to which we have dedicated ourselves in the four years that have passed: family, birth rate, tax reduction, protection of purchasing power, work incentives, business incentives, citizens' health
Giorgia Meloni
Possiamo già affermare che questa manovra non offre risposte adeguate all'emergenza economica e sociale del Paese. Le misure previste non incidono sulle cause dell'impoverimento, non restituiscono potere d'acquisto ai salari e non rafforzano in modo strutturale i servizi pubblici.
We can already state that this maneuver does not offer adequate responses to the country's economic and social emergency. The planned measures do not address the causes of impoverishment, do not restore purchasing power to wages, and do not structurally strengthen public services.
Italian Communist Party

IT2026-11-04#
party document Public statement

Elezioni di Midterm negli Stati Uniti

The Italian Communist Party issued a statement on the 2026 US midterm elections, arguing that while the Democratic victory signals discontent with Trump's policies—especially regarding the Iran conflict and inflation—it does not represent a true alternative, and calling for Europe and Italy to pursue diplomatic solutions and prioritize workers' interests over alignment with US strategy.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Trump aveva conquistato una parte importante dell'elettorato, soprattutto quello MAGA, promettendo di mettere l'America al primo posto, difendere il potere d'acquisto delle famiglie e tenere gli Stati Uniti lontani da nuove guerre.
Trump had won an important part of the electorate, especially the MAGA one, promising to put America first, defend families' purchasing power and keep the United States away from new wars.
Partito Comunista Italiano
Democratici e Repubblicani rappresentano ancora, su molte questioni fondamentali, due facce della stessa medaglia: quella di un sistema politico ed economico dominato dagli interessi del grande capitale, segnato dalle disuguaglianze sociali e dal mantenimento della potenza globale statunitense.
Democrats and Republicans still represent, on many fundamental issues, two sides of the same coin: that of a political and economic system dominated by the interests of big capital, marked by social inequalities and the maintenance of US global power.
Partito Comunista Italiano

IT2026-11-03#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Mozione PCI: approvata “Diritto di scelta”

The Italian Communist Party's motion on preventive conscientious objection was approved by the Assembly of Municipalities of Circondario Empolese Valdelsa, with the right voting against, following the model of Capaci municipality which allows 17-year-olds to declare conscientious objector status preventively.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
È STATA APPROVATA (LA DESTRA HA VOTATO CONTRO) LA MOZIONE 'DIRITTO DI SCELTA', PRESENTATA DAL GRUPPO PCI DELL'ASSEMBLEA DEI COMUNI CIRCONDARIO EMPOLESE VALDELSA.
THE MOTION 'RIGHT OF CHOICE' WAS APPROVED (THE RIGHT VOTED AGAINST), PRESENTED BY THE PCI GROUP OF THE ASSEMBLY OF MUNICIPALITIES CIRCONDARIO EMPOLESE VALDELSA.
Italian Communist Party
Con una delibera di Giunta, il Comune di Capaci, la delibera n°80 del 6 maggio 2026, ha introdotto il principio della obiezione di coscienza preventiva, invitando tutti i diciassettenni (e i loro genitori, trattandosi di minorenni) ad inviare all'ufficio comunale un'Istanza, con cui comunicano di dichiararsi obiettori di coscienza.
With a council resolution, the Municipality of Capaci, resolution no. 80 of May 6, 2026, introduced the principle of preventive conscientious objection, inviting all seventeen-year-olds (and their parents, being minors) to send to the municipal office a request, with which they communicate their declaration as conscientious objectors.
Susanna Rovai

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

SVs alternative budsjett: – En vanlig jobb skal være nok til å leve gode liv

The Socialist Left Party presented its alternative budget for 2027, proposing major spending increases on housing construction, welfare, climate measures, and tax cuts for lower incomes, funded partly by higher wealth taxes on fortunes over 100 million kroner.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

People will be better off with our budget
Marthe Hammer
Vi lever i en tid med voksende ulikhet, en mer urolig verden og en klima- og naturkrise med allerede enorme konsekvenser. De siste årene har hverdagen blitt tøffere for mange av oss. We live in a time of growing inequality, a more turbulent world and a climate and nature crisis with already enormous consequences. In recent years, everyday life has become tougher for many of us. Politics must show that it is possible to give people a better and safer everyday life. We do that with our alternative budget.
Marthe Hammer

Partido Comunista Português

The Portuguese Communist Party issued a statement on the US midterm elections, characterizing the Republican Party's defeat as a repudiation of Trump's reactionary and warmongering policies, particularly regarding military aggression against Iran, support for Israel's actions in Palestine, and policies against Cuba, while calling for continued solidarity with Palestine and Cuba.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
A clara derrota do Partido Republicano nas eleições intercalares nos EUA, nomeadamente com a perda da maioria no Senado e na Câmara dos Representantes, é expressão do amplo descontentamento e repúdio por parte de diversos sectores da sociedade norte-americana face à política reaccionária e belicista da Administração Trump e às suas nefastas consequências.

Partido Comunista Português

PT2026-11-04#
party document Public statement

Partido Comunista Português

PCP Secretary-General Paulo Raimundo condemned the government's housing policy, criticized the sale of TAP to Lufthansa, denounced a coalition between PSD, PS, Chega, CDS and IL on eviction laws and rent increases, and called for protests on November 14 and 21, 2026.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
É extraordinário que no exacto dia em que o Governo entrega a TAP à Luftansa, com a TAP a ser novamente comprada com o próprio dinheiro da TAP, no dia em que o CDS serviu de moço de recados de PSD, Chega e IL, travando a possibilidade do debate de urgência na AR requerido pelo PCP, é exactamente neste dia que o Governo pela voz do Primeiro-Ministro decide fazer uma comunicação ao País onde reafirma todo um caminho desastroso relativamente à habitação.
It is extraordinary that on the exact day the Government hands TAP over to Lufthansa, with TAP being bought again with TAP's own money, on the day CDS served as errand boy for PSD, Chega and IL, blocking the possibility of urgent debate in the Assembly requested by PCP, it is exactly on this day that the Government through the voice of the Prime Minister decides to make a communication to the country reaffirming a disastrous path regarding housing.
Paulo Raimundo
Uma opção do Governo de envolvimento dos seus dois maiores parceiros, um PS que viabiliza o Orçamento do Estado, um Chega que garante a lei dos despejos, uma política contra a maioria que quer continuar.
A Government option involving its two biggest partners, a PS that enables the State Budget, a Chega that guarantees the eviction law, a policy against the majority that wants to continue.
Paulo Raimundo

PT2026-11-03#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Partido Comunista Português

The Portuguese Communist Party presented budget amendment proposals for the 2027 State Budget, including raising corporate tax to 21%, creating a new 10th IRS bracket at 56% for incomes over 250,000 euros, reducing VAT on essential goods to 6%, and rescuing public-private partnerships in infrastructure.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Mais de 72% das receitas fiscais são hoje suportadas pelo IVA e pelo IRS, ou seja, é quem trabalha e trabalhou a suportar o grosso dos impostos no nosso país, enquanto os rendimentos do capital veem o seu contributo cada vez mais reduzido
More than 72% of tax revenues are today supported by VAT and IRS, that is, it is those who work and have worked who bear the bulk of taxes in our country, while capital income sees its contribution increasingly reduced
Paula Santos
Propomos a reposição nos 21% da taxa de IRC (cada ponto percentual custa, pelo menos, 300 M€ segundo o Governo) e a eliminação dos benefícios fiscais previstos no IRC (1886 M€)
We propose restoring the IRC rate to 21% (each percentage point costs at least 300 million euros according to the Government) and eliminating the tax benefits provided for in IRC (1886 million euros)
Paula Santos
